# Graph Report - floem  (2026-10-07)

## Corpus Check
- 2326 files · ~1,945,195 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 37257 nodes · 83666 edges · 1526 communities (1247 shown, 108 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1615 edges (avg confidence: 0.85)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `39f72a33`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
